# Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 1011/2014 of 22 September 2014 laying down detailed rules for implementing Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council as regards the models for submission of certain information to the Commission and the detailed rules concerning the exchanges of information between beneficiaries and managing authorities, certifying authorities, audit authorities and intermediate bodies

> Text as at 2020-12-31.
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, Northern Ireland.

*Extent: E+W+S+N.I.*

Commission Implementing Regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] (EU) No 1011/2014 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

of 22 September 2014 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

laying down detailed rules for implementing Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council as regards the models for submission of certain information to the Commission and the detailed rules concerning the exchanges of information between beneficiaries and managing authorities, certifying authorities, audit authorities and intermediate bodies *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

THE EUROPEAN COMMISSION, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

Having regard to the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

Having regard to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 17 December 2013 laying down common provisions on the European Regional Development Fund, the European Social Fund, the Cohesion Fund, the European Agricultural Fund for Rural Development and the European Maritime and Fisheries Fund and laying down general provisions on the European Regional Development Fund, the European Social Fund, the Cohesion Fund and the European Maritime and Fisheries Fund and repealing Council Regulation (EC) No 1083/2006 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00001], and in particular Articles 41(4), 102(1), 112(5), 122(3), 124(7), 131(6) and 137(3) thereof, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

Whereas: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(1)

Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 184/2014 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00002] lays down provisions necessary for the preparation of programmes. In order to ensure implementation of the programmes financed by the European Structural and Investment Funds (the ‘ESI Funds’), it is necessary to lay down further provisions for the application of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. To facilitate a comprehensive view and the access to those provisions, those provisions should be set out in one implementing act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(2)

In order to ensure consistency and quality of the information submitted by the managing authority to the Commission, including clear statements on the investment's feasibility and economic viability of the major project, a standard format laying down uniform requirements for the structure and the content of the information to notify selected major projects to the Commission should be established. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(3)

With a view to ensuring increased efficiency and transparency in the implementation of programmes financed by the ESI Funds, the model for transmission of financial data, the model for payment applications and, pursuant to Article 41(4) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, the model to be used when submitting additional information concerning financial instruments with the application for payments to the Commission, as well as the model for the accounts for operational programmes should be established. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(4)

For the same purpose, the model for describing the functions and procedures in place in the managing authority and, where appropriate, the certifying authority, and the model for the report and opinion of the independent audit body should be established. They should set out the technical characteristics of each field in the electronic data exchange system. Since those models will form the basis for developing the electronic data exchange system referred to in Article 74(4) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, they should also set out the manner in which data on eligible expenditure will be entered into this system for electronic information exchange. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(5)

Detailed rules need to be set out on the scope and characteristics of the systems supporting electronic exchange of information between beneficiaries and a managing authority, a certifying authority, an audit authority and intermediate bodies so that Member States are given legal certainty on the obligations they must fulfil at the regulatory deadline set out in Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(6)

To reduce the administrative burden for beneficiaries in an effective, efficient and satisfactory way for them while ensuring the efficient, effective and secure electronic exchange of information, certain basic requirements and technical characteristics should be specified for the systems referred to in Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(7)

Principles and applicable rules should be set out regarding the transmission of documents and data through the systems relating to identifying of the party responsible for uploading the documents and any updates thereto including an alternative solution in the event that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] *force majeure *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]* hinders the use of the systems. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(8)

This Regulation should respect the fundamental rights and observe the principles recognised by the Charter of Fundamental Rights of the European Union, and notably the right to protection of personal data. This Regulation should therefore be applied in accordance with these rights and principles. Concerning personal data processed by Member States, Directive 95/46/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00003] applies. Concerning the processing of personal data by the Union institutions and bodies and the free movement of such data, Regulation (EC) No 45/2001 of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00004] applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

(9)

The measures provided for in this Regulation are in accordance with the opinion of the Coordination Committee for the European Structural and Investment Funds, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

HAS ADOPTED THIS REGULATION: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

## CHAPTER I (affected by repeal, see note) — MODELS AND FORMAT TO BE USED FOR SUBMISSION OF CERTAIN INFORMATION TO THE COMMISSION (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 1 (affected by repeal, see note). Format for notification of a selected major project (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The notification of a selected major project by the managing authority to the Commission in accordance with the first subparagraph of Article 102(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall be drawn up in accordance with the format set out in Annex I to this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 2 (affected by repeal, see note). Model for transmission of financial data (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

When submitting financial data to the Commission for monitoring purposes in accordance with Article 112 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, the Member States shall use the model set out in Annex II to this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 3 (affected by repeal, see note). Model for the description of the functions and procedures in place for the managing authority and the certifying authority (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1. The description of the functions and procedures in place for the managing authority and, where appropriate, the certifying authority shall be drawn up in accordance with the model set out in Annex III to this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2. Where a common system applies to several operational programmes, a single description of the functions and procedures referred to in paragraph 1 may be drawn up. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 4 (affected by repeal, see note). Model for the report of the independent audit body (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1. The audit report of the independent audit body referred to in Article 124(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall be drawn up in accordance with the model set out in Annex IV to this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2. Where a common system applies to several operational programmes, a single audit report referred to in paragraph 1 may be drawn up. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 5 (affected by repeal, see note). Model for opinion of the independent audit body (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1. The opinion of the independent audit body referred to in Article 124(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall be drawn up in accordance with the model set out in Annex V to this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2. Where a common system applies to several operational programmes, a single opinion referred to in paragraph 1 may be drawn up. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 6 (affected by repeal, see note). Model for the payment application including additional information concerning financial instruments (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The payment application referred to in Articles 41(4) and 131(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall be drawn up in accordance with the model set out in Annex VI to this Regulation and shall include, where applicable, additional information concerning financial instruments. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 7 (affected by repeal, see note). Model for the accounts (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The accounts referred to in Article 137(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall be submitted to the Commission in accordance with the model set out in Annex VII to this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

## CHAPTER II (affected by repeal, see note) — DETAILED RULES CONCERNING THE EXCHANGES OF INFORMATION BETWEEN BENEFICIARIES AND MANAGING AUTHORITIES, CERTIFYING AUTHORITIES, AUDIT AUTHORITIES AND INTERMEDIATE BODIES (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 8 (affected by repeal, see note). Definition and scope of electronic data exchange systems (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1. ‘Electronic data exchange systems’, as referred to in the first subparagraph of Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 shall mean mechanisms and instruments allowing the electronic exchange of documents and data, including audiovisual media supports, scanned documents and electronic files. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The exchange of documents and data shall include reporting on progress, payment claims and exchange of information related to management verifications and audits. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2. The electronic data exchange systems shall enable administrative verification in respect of each application for reimbursement by beneficiaries under Article 125(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and audits to rely on information and documents available through the electronic data exchange systems, when such information and documents are exchanged in electronic form in compliance with Article 122(3) of that regulation. Paper documents may only be requested by these responsible authorities in exceptional cases, following a risk analysis, and only if paper documents are the true source of the scanned documents uploaded in the electronic data exchange systems. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 9 (affected by repeal, see note). Characteristics of the electronic data exchange systems (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1. The electronic data exchange systems shall ensure data security, data integrity, data confidentiality, authentication of the sender in accordance with Articles 122(3), 125(4)(d), 125(8) and 140 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The electronic data exchange systems shall be available and operational during and outside standard office hours, except for technical maintenance activities. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2. If a Member State, on its own initiative, imposes a compulsory use of electronic data exchange systems upon beneficiaries, it shall ensure that the technical characteristics of those systems will not disrupt smooth implementation of the Funds nor restrict access for any beneficiaries. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

This requirement does not apply to electronic data exchange systems for beneficiaries which were made compulsory by a Member State during a previous programming period and comply with other requirements laid down in this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3. The electronic data exchange systems shall be equipped with at least the following functionalities: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

- (a) interactive forms and/or forms prefilled by the system on the basis of the data which are stored at consecutive steps of the procedures; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- (b) automatic calculations where applicable; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- (c) automatic embedded controls which reduce repeated exchanges of documents or information as far as possible; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- (d) system-generated alerts to inform the beneficiary that certain actions can be performed; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- (e) online status tracking allowing the beneficiary to monitor the current status of the project; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- (f) availability of all previous data and documents processed by the electronic data exchange system. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

### Article 10 (affected by repeal, see note). Transmission of documents and data through the electronic data exchange systems (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1. The beneficiaries and the authorities referred to in the first subparagraph of Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, shall add the documents and data for which they are responsible, and any updates thereto, to the electronic data exchange systems in the electronic format defined by the Member State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The Member State shall lay down detailed terms and conditions of electronic data exchange in the document setting out the conditions for support for each operation referred to in Article 125(3)(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2. Exchanges of data and transactions shall bear an electronic signature compatible with one of the three types of electronic signature defined by Directive 1999/93/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00005].

3. The date of transmission of documents and data by the beneficiary to the authorities referred to in the first subparagraph of Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and vice versa shall be considered to be the date of electronic submission of the information which is stored in the electronic data exchange systems. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

4. Submission of documents and data through the electronic data exchange systems shall be made only once as referred to in the second subparagraph of Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 as regards the same operation for all authorities implementing the same programme. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

These authorities shall work together at legal, organisational, semantic and technical levels ensuring effective communication, as well as the exchange and re-use of information and knowledge. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

This is without prejudice of processes allowing the beneficiary to update erroneous or obsolete data or unreadable documents. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

5. The electronic data exchange systems shall be accessible either directly through an interactive user interface (a web application) or via a technical interface that allows for automatic synchronisation and transmission of data between beneficiaries' and Member States' systems. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

6. When processing information, the electronic data exchange systems shall guarantee the protection of privacy of personal data for individuals and commercial confidentiality for legal entities according to Directive 2002/58/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00006], Directive 2009/136/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00007] and Directive 95/46/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00008].

7. In cases of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] *force majeure *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]*, and in particular of malfunctioning of the electronic data exchange systems or a lack of a lasting data connection, the beneficiary concerned may submit the information required to the competent authorities in the forms and using the means determined by the Member State for such cases. As soon as the cause of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] *force majeure *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]* ceases, the Member State shall ensure that the relevant documents are integrated into the database related to the electronic data exchange systems. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

By way of derogation from paragraph 3, the date taken into account for submitting the required information shall be deemed to be the date of sending of the documents in the required forms. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

8. Member States shall ensure that all beneficiaries can use the electronic data exchange systems referred to in Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, including the beneficiaries of ongoing operations at the date on which the electronic data exchange systems become operational and to which electronic data exchange applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

**Article 11 (affected by repeal, see note).**[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] This Regulation shall enter into force on the twentieth day following that of its publication in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] *Official Journal of the European Union *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]*.

This Regulation shall be binding in its entirety and directly applicable in all Member States. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

## ANNEX I (affected by repeal, see note) — Format for the notification of a selected major project (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

**NOTIFICATION TO THE COMMISSION OF THE SELECTED MAJOR PROJECT IN ACCORDANCE WITH THE FIRST SUBPARAGRAPH OF ARTICLE 102(1) OF REGULATION (EU) No 1303/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

**EUROPEAN REGIONAL DEVELOPMENT FUND/COHESION FUND *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

**Project title \[‘’\] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

**CCI \[\] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

A.

BODY RESPONSIBLE FOR IMPLEMENTATION OF THE MAJOR PROJECT *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

**Authority responsible for project notification (managing authority or intermediate body) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

## ANNEX III (affected by repeal, see note) — Model for the description of the functions and procedures in place for the managing authority and the certifying authority (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1\.

GENERAL *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.1.

**Information submitted by: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

- \[Name of the\] Member State; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Title of the programme and CCI: (all operational programmes covered by the managing authority/certifying authority), in case of common management and control system); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Name of main contact point, including e-mail: (body responsible for the description). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.2.

**The information provided describes the situation on: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]** (dd/mm/yy) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.3.

**System structure *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]** (general information and flowchart showing the organisational relationship between the authorities/bodies involved in the management and control system) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.3.1.

Managing authority (Name, address and contact point in the managing authority): *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

Indicate whether the managing authority is also designated as the certifying authority, in accordance with Article 123(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.3.2.

Certifying authority (Name, address and contact point in the certifying authority). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.3.3.

Intermediate bodies (Name, address and contact points in the intermediate bodies). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.3.4.

When Article 123(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 applies, indicate how the principle of separation of functions between the audit authority and the managing/certifying authorities is ensured. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2\.

MANAGING AUTHORITY *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.1.

**Managing authority and its main functions *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

2.1.1.

The status of the managing authority (national, regional or local public body or private body) and the body of which it is part *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00042].

2.1.2.

Specification of the functions and tasks carried out directly by the managing authority. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

Where the managing authority also carries out in addition the functions of the certifying authority, description of how separation of functions is ensured. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.1.3.

Specification of the functions formally delegated by the managing authority, identification of the intermediate bodies and the form of the delegation (underlying that the managing authorities maintains the full responsibility for the delegated functions), under Article 123(6) and (7) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. Reference to relevant documents (legal acts with empowerments, agreements). Where applicable, specifications of the functions of the controllers foreseen in Article 23(4) of Regulation (EU) No 1299/2013, for European territorial cooperation programmes. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.1.4.

Description of the procedures for ensuring effective and proportionate anti-fraud measures taking account of the risks identified, including reference to the risk assessment carried out (Article 125(4)(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.2.

**Organisation and procedures of the managing authority *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

2.2.1.

Organisation chart and specifications of the functions of the units (including the plan for allocation of appropriate human resources with the necessary skills). This information also covers the intermediate bodies to which some functions have been delegated. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.2.2.

Framework to ensure that an appropriate risk management exercise is conducted when necessary, and in particular in the event of major modifications to the management and control system. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.2.3.

Description of the following procedures (that should be provided in writing to the staff of the managing authority and intermediate bodies; date and reference): *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

- Procedures to support the work of the monitoring committee. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures for a system to collect, record and store in computerised form data on each operation necessary for monitoring, evaluation, financial management, verification and audit, including, where applicable, data on individual participants and a breakdown of data on indicators by gender when required. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures for the supervision of the functions formally delegated by the managing authority under Article 123(6) and (7) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures for appraising, selecting and approving operations and for ensuring their compliance, for the entire implementation period, with applicable rules (Article 125(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013), including instructions and guidance ensuring the contribution of operations to achieving the specific objectives and results of the relevant priorities in accordance with the provisions of Article 125(3)(a)(i) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and procedures to ensure that operations are not selected where they have been physically completed or fully implemented before the application for funding by the beneficiary (including the procedures used by the intermediate bodies where the appraisal, selection and approval of operations have been delegated). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures to ensure the provision to the beneficiary of a document setting out the conditions for support for each operation, including procedures to ensure that beneficiaries maintain either a separate accounting system or an adequate accounting code for all transactions relating to an operation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures for the verifications of operations (in line with requirements under Article 125(4) to (7) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013), including for ensuring the compliance of operations with the Union policies (such as those related to partnership and multi-level governance, promotion of equality between men and women, non-discrimination, accessibility for persons with disabilities, sustainable development, public procurement, State aid and environment rules), and identification of the authorities or bodies carrying out such verifications. The description shall cover administrative management verifications in respect of each application for reimbursement by beneficiaries and on-the-spot management verifications of operations, that may be carried out on a sample basis. Where the management verifications have been delegated to intermediate bodies, the description should include the procedures applied by the intermediate bodies for those verifications and the procedures applied by the managing authority to supervise the effectiveness of the functions delegated to the intermediate bodies. The frequency and coverage shall be proportionate to the amount of public support to an operation and to the level of risk identified by these verifications and audits by the audit authority for the management and control system as a whole. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Description of the procedures by which applications for reimbursement are received from beneficiaries, verified, and validated, and by which payments to beneficiaries are authorised, executed and accounted for, in line with obligations set out in Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 as from 2016 (including the procedures used by the intermediate bodies where processing of applications for reimbursement has been delegated), in view of respecting the deadline of 90 days for payments to beneficiaries under Article 132 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Identification of the authorities or bodies carrying out each step in the processing of the application for reimbursement, including a flowchart indicating all bodies involved. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Description of how information is transmitted to the certifying authority by the managing authority, including information on deficiencies and/or irregularities (including suspected and established fraud) detected and their follow-up in the context of management verifications, audits and controls by Union or national bodies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Description of how information is transmitted to the audit authority by the managing authority, including information on deficiencies and/or irregularities (including suspected and established fraud) detected and their follow-up in the context of management verifications, audits and controls by Union or national bodies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Reference to national eligibility rules laid down by the Member State and applicable to the operational programme. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures to draw up and submit to the Commission annual and final implementation reports (Article 125(2)(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013), including the procedures for collecting and reporting reliable data on performance indicators (Article 125(2)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures for drawing up the management declaration (Article 125(4)(e) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures for drawing up the annual summary of the final audit reports and of controls carried out, including an analysis of the nature and extent of errors and weaknesses identified in systems, as well as corrective action taken or planned (Article 125(4)(e) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Procedures concerning the communication to staff of the above procedures, as well as an indication of training organised/foreseen and any guidance issued (date and reference). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Description, where applicable, of the procedures of the managing authority in relation to the scope, rules and procedures concerning the effective arrangements set out by the Member State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00043] for the examination of complaints concerning the ESI Funds, in the context of Article 74(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.3.

**Audit trail *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

2.3.1.

Procedures to ensure an adequate audit trail and archiving system, including with respect to the security of data, taking account of Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, in accordance with national rules on the certification of conformity of documents (Article 125(4)(d) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and Article 25 of Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) No 480/2014). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.3.2.

Instructions given on keeping supporting documents available by beneficiaries/intermediate bodies/managing authority (date and reference): *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

- Indication of the period during which documents are to be held. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Format in which the documents are to be held. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.4.

**Irregularities and recoveries *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

2.4.1.

Description of the procedure (that should be provided in writing to the staff of the managing authority and intermediate bodies: date and reference) on reporting and correction of irregularities (including fraud) and their follow-up and recording of amounts withdrawn and recovered, amounts to be recovered, irrecoverable amounts and amounts related to operations suspended by a legal proceeding or by an administrative appeal having suspensory effect. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.4.2.

Description of the procedure (including a flowchart setting out the reporting lines) to comply with the obligation to notify irregularities to the Commission in accordance with Article 122(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3\.

CERTIFYING AUTHORITY *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.1.

**Certifying authority and its main functions *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

3.1.1.

The status of the certifying authority (national, regional or local public body) and the body of which it is part. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.1.2.

Specification of the functions carried out by the certifying authority. Where the managing authority also carries out in addition the functions of the certifying authority, description of how separation of functions is ensured (see 2.1.2). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.1.3.

Functions formally delegated by the certifying authority, identification of the intermediate bodies and the form of the delegation under Article 123(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. Reference to relevant documents (legal acts with empowerments, agreements). Description of the procedures used by the intermediate bodies to carry out delegated tasks, and of the procedures of the certifying authority to supervise the effectiveness of the tasks delegated to the intermediate bodies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.2.

**Organisation of the certifying authority *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

3.2.1.

Organisation chart and specification of the functions of the units (including plan for allocation of appropriate human resources with necessary skills). This information also covers the intermediate bodies to which some tasks have been delegated). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.2.2.

Description of the procedures to be provided in writing to the staff of the certifying authority and intermediate bodies (date and reference): *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

- Procedures for drawing up and submitting payment applications: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
  - Description of arrangements in place for the certifying authority to access any information on operations, necessary for the purpose of drawing up and submitting payment applications, including the results of management verifications (in line with Article 125 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013) and all relevant audits. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
  - Description of the procedure by which payment applications are drawn up and submitted to the Commission, including procedure to ensure sending of the final application for interim payment by 31 July following the end of the previous accounting year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Description of the accounting system used as a basis for certification of expenditure accounts to the Commission (Article 126(d) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013): *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
  - arrangements for forwarding aggregated data to the certifying authority in case of a decentralised system, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
  - the link between the accounting system and the information system described under paragraph 4.1, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
  - identification of European Structural and Investment Fund transactions in case of a common system with other Funds. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Description of the procedures in place for drawing up the accounts referred to in Article 59(5) of Regulation (EU, Euratom) No 966/2012 (Article 126(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013). Arrangements for certifying the completeness, accuracy and veracity of the accounts and that the expenditure entered in the accounts complies with applicable law (Article 126(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013) taking into account the results of all verifications and audits. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Description, where applicable, of the procedures of the certifying authority in relation to the scope, rules and procedures concerning the effective arrangements set out by the Member State *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00044] for the examination of complaints concerning the ESI Funds, in the context of Article 74(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.3.

**Recoveries *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

3.3.1.

Description of the system for ensuring prompt recovery of public assistance, including Union assistance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.3.2.

Procedures for ensuring an adequate audit trail by maintaining accounting records in computerised form, including amounts recovered, amounts to be recovered, amounts withdrawn from a payment application, amounts irrecoverable and amounts related to operations suspended by a legal proceeding or by an administrative appeal having suspensory effect, for each operation, including the recoveries resulting from the application of Article 71 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 on durability of operations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3.3.3.

Arrangements for deducting amounts recovered or amounts to be withdrawn from expenditure to be declared. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

4\.

INFORMATION SYSTEM *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

4.1.

**Description of the information systems including a flowchart (central or common network system or decentralised system with links between the systems) for: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

- Collecting, recording and storing, in a computerised form data on each operation, including where appropriate data on individual participants and a breakdown of data on indicators by gender when required, necessary for monitoring, evaluation, financial management, verification and audit, as required by Article 125(2)(d) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and by Article 24 of Commission Delegated Regulation (EC) No 480/2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Ensuring that the data referred to in the previous point is collected, entered and stored in the system, and that data on indicators is broken down by gender where required by Annexes I and II to Regulation (EU) No 1304/2013, as required by Article 125(2)(e) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Ensuring that there is a system which records and stores, in computerised form, accounting records for each operation, and which supports all the data required for drawing up payment applications and accounts, including records of amounts to be recovered, amounts recovered, amounts irrecoverable and amounts withdrawn following cancellation of all or part of the contribution for an operation or operational programme, as set out in Article 126(d) and 137(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Maintaining accounting records in a computerised form of expenditure declared to the Commission and the corresponding public contribution paid to beneficiaries, as set out in Article 126(g) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Keeping an account of amounts recoverable and of amounts withdrawn following cancellation of all or part of the contribution for an operation, as set out in Article 126(h) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Keeping records of amounts related to operations suspended by a legal proceeding or by an administrative appeal having suspensory effects. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Indication as to whether the systems are operational and can reliably record the data mentioned above. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

4.2.

**Description of the procedures to verify that IT systems security is ensured. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

4.3.

**Description of the current situation as regards implementation of the requirements of Article 122(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

## ANNEX IV (affected by repeal, see note) — Model for the report of an independent audit body under Article 124(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1\.

**Introduction *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

1.1

Identify the objective of the report, i.e. to set out the results of the assessment of the compliance of the managing authority and the certifying authority with the designation criteria relating to internal control environment, risk management, management and control activities and monitoring set out in Annex XIII to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, in order to express an opinion on their compliance with the designation criteria. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.2

Identify the scope of the report i.e. the body(ies) covered, namely the managing authority and the certifying authority (and, where appropriate, the delegated functions of these authorities) and their compliance with the designation criteria relating to internal control environment, risk management, management and control activities and monitoring set out in Annex XIII to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, with reference to the particular Funds and programmes covered. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.3

Indicate the body that has prepared the report (‘Independent Audit Body’) and specify if it is the audit authority for the operational programme(s) covered. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

1.4

Indicate how the functional independence of the Independent Audit Body from the managing and certifying authorities is ensured (see Article 124(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013). Where applicable, indicate also how the functional independence of the Independent Audit Body from the joint secretariat (foreseen in Article 23(2) of the Regulation (EU) No 1299/2013) is ensured. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2\.

**Methodology and scope of the work *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

2.1

Indicate the period and timeframe of the audit (date when the final description of functions and procedures in place for the managing authority and, where appropriate, the certifying authority was received by the Independent Audit Body, date when the audit started and ended and resources allocated). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.2

Specify the extent of the use of previous audit work for the 2007-2013 programming period, where applicable, in accordance with Article 124(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.3

Specify a) the extent of the use of audit work carried out by other bodies and b) the quality control performed on such audit work with respect to the adequacy of the work. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.4

Describe the work done for assessing, in line with Article 124(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, the fulfilment, by the managing and certifying authorities being designated by \[Member State\], of the criteria relating to the internal control environment, risk management, management and control activities, and monitoring set out in Annex XIII of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, covering among other elements, the following: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

- Examination of the description of functions and procedures in place for the managing authority and, where appropriate, the certifying authority, in accordance with the model defined in Annex III of this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Examination of other relevant documents concerning the system; indicate any review of laws, ministerial acts, circulars, internal procedure/other manuals, guidelines and/or checklists. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Interviews with the staff in the main bodies (including intermediate bodies, if relevant). Include description of the method and criteria for selection, what subjects have been covered, how many interviews have taken place and who has been interviewed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Review of the description and procedures relating to the information systems, covering in particular the requirements set out in Annex XIII of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 and the verification of whether these systems are operational and have been set-up in order to ensure: (i) an adequate audit trail; (ii) protection of personal data; (iii) integrity, availability and authenticity of data; (iv) reliable, accurate and complete information on the implementation of the operational programme (in line with Article 125(2)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013), data on each operation necessary for monitoring, evaluation, financial management, verification and audit (in line with Article 125(2)(d) and (e) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013) and data required for drawing up payment applications and accounts (as required by Article 126 (d) (g) and (h) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- Where functions have been delegated by the managing authority or the certifying authority to other bodies, describe the audit work done to verify that the managing and/or certifying authority have assessed the capacities of these bodies to carry out delegated tasks, that they have sufficient supervisory procedures in place over these intermediate bodies and any other relevant audit work. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.5

Indicate if any contradictory procedures have taken place prior to issuing this report and indicate the relevant authorities/bodies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.6

Confirm that the work has been carried out taking account of internationally accepted audit standards. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

2.7

Identify if there were any limitation of scope *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00045], in particular the ones with affecting the opinion of the independent audit body. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

3\.

**Results of assessment for each authority/system *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]**

3.1.

For each authority/system complete the table: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

| CCI or system (group of CCIs) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Concerned Authority(Managing or Certifying authority) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Completeness and accuracy of description (Y/N) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Conclusion (unqualified, qualified, adverse) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Designation criteria affected *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Section of description of functions and procedures affected *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Shortcomings *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Priorities affected *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Recommendations/Corrective measures *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] | Timeframe agreed with concerned authority for implementation of corrective measures *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9] |

| **System y *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]** | **Managing authority *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]** |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |

3.2.

Provide results of the assessment on areas not fully covered in the table above, including but not limited to: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

- The procedures in place for drawing up the accounts referred to in Article 59(5)(a) of Regulation (EU, Euratom) No 966/2012 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00046] (Article 126(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- The arrangements for certifying the completeness, accuracy and veracity of the accounts and that the expenditure entered in the accounts complies with applicable law and has been incurred in respect of operations selected for funding in accordance with the criteria applicable to the operational programme and complying with applicable law (Article 126(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- The procedures in place for ensuring effective and proportionate anti-fraud measures taking account of the risks identified (Article 125(4)(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- The framework to ensure that an appropriate risk management exercise is conducted when necessary, and in particular in the event of major modifications to the management and control system (Annex XIII, point 2, of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- The arrangements for drawing up the management declaration and annual summary of final audits and controls and weaknesses identified (Article 125(4)(e) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- The arrangements for collecting, recording and storing, in computerised form, data on each operation necessary for monitoring, evaluation, financial management, verification and audit, including data on indicators and outputs (Article 125(2)(d) and (e) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- The framework for ensuring, in the event of delegation of tasks to intermediate bodies, the definition of their respective responsibilities and obligations, the verification of their capacities to carry out delegated tasks and the existence of reporting procedures (Annex XIII, point 1(ii), of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

## ANNEX V (affected by repeal, see note) — Model for the opinion of an independent audit body on the compliance of the managing authority and the certifying authority with the designation criteria set out in Annex XIII to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

To (Member State authority/body) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

INTRODUCTION *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

I, the undersigned, representing \[name of the independent audit body under Article 124(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013\] as the body functionally independent from the managing and certifying authorities, responsible for drawing up a report and opinion setting out the results of an assessment of the compliance of the managing authority and the certifying authority with the designation criteria set out in Annex XIII to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 for \[name of operational programme(s), CCI code(s)\] (hereinafter ‘the programme(s)’), have carried out an examination in accordance with Article 124(2) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

SCOPE OF THE EXAMINATION *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The examination covered the managing authority, the certifying authority and (where appropriate) the delegated functions of these authorities, as described in section 1 of the attached report \[Annex IV of this Regulation\] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The extent and scope of the examination is detailed in section 2 of the attached report. Among other aspects described in this report, the examination was based on the description of the functions and procedures in place for the managing authority and, where appropriate, the certifying authority, drawn-up by and under the responsibility of.\[name of body or bodies responsible for the description\] and received on \[ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]*dd/mm/yyyy *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]*\] from \[name of body or bodies submitting the description\]. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

For the part of the management and control system *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00047] in \[the managing authority or certifying authority\] relating to …, I have concluded that the system is essentially the same as for the previous programming period and that there is evidence, on the basis of audit work done in accordance with the relevant provisions of Council Regulation (EC) No 1083/2006 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00048] or Council Regulation (EC) No 1198/2006 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00049], of their effective functioning during that period. I have therefore concluded that the relevant criteria are fulfilled without carrying out additional audit work. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

OPINION *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

***(Unqualified opinion) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]***

On the basis of the examination referred to above, it is my opinion that the managing authority and/or the certifying authority being designated for the programme(s) comply with the designation criteria relating to internal control environment, risk management, management and control activities and monitoring set out in Annex XIII to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

***Or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]***

***(Qualified opinion) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]***

On the basis of the examination referred to above, it is my opinion that the managing authority and/or the certifying authority being designated for the programme(s) comply with the designation criteria relating to internal control environment, risk management, management and control activities and monitoring set out in Annex XIII to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013, except in the following respects *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00050]

My reasons for considering that this/these authority(ies) do(es) not comply with the designation criterion(a) and my assessment of the seriousness are as follows *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00051]:

***Or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]***

***(Adverse opinion) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]***

On the basis of the examination referred to above, it is my opinion that the managing authority and/or the certifying authority being designated for the programme(s) do not comply with the designation criteria relating to internal control environment, risk management, management and control activities and monitoring set out in Annex XIII to Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

This adverse opinion is based *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

on *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9][^f00052]

The model is automatically adjusted on basis of the CCI. The certifying authority hereby certifies that: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

- 1.. that the accounts are complete accurate and true and that the expenditure entered into the accounts complies with applicable law and has been incurred in respect of operations selected for funding in accordance with the criteria applicable to the operational programme and complying with applicable law; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- 2.. that the provisions in the Fund-specific Regulations, Article 59(5) of Regulation (EU, Euratom) No 966/2012 and in points (d) and (f) of Article 126 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 are respected; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
- 3.. that the provisions in Article 140 of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 with regard to the availability of documents are respected. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]

The model is automatically adjusted on basis of the CCI. The model is automatically adjusted on basis of the CCI. As an example, in case of programmes not including categories of regions (Cohesion Fund, ETC, EMFF, ENI and IPA contributions to ETC, YEI dedicated operational programme without technical assistance, if applicable) the table shall look as follows: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-69fd3d1df62ef917abd8f1bee85dd8b9]
